    """Operational status from actual run artifacts; never a convergence decision."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import datetime,json,time,sys
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(p.read_text())
    def update():
        lines=['# Core-filter work in progress','',f"Updated {datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')}.",'','The stable, calibrated 00:11 filter goal remains unfinished. This page reports actual fixed-run progress; completion is not a convergence certificate.','', '| Comparison | Actual progress | Result or purpose |','|---|---|---|']
        configs=[('Original branching, larger populations','extended-scaling',4,320000),('Defensive command mixture, branching','defensive-command-mixture/branching-comparison',8,None),('MH371 physical known flight','defensive-command-mixture/known-flight',8,None),('Current canonical runner','canonical-comparison',8,None)]
        complete=True
        for label,folder,count,n in configs:
            p=B/folder;done=(p/'completed.json').exists();complete &= done
            receipts=list((p/'runs').glob('*.execution.json'));receipts=[x for x in receipts if x.stem"'!='"'pilot.execution']
            successes=sum(read(x)['returncode']==0 for x in receipts)
            if n:
                roots=sum(read(x).get('completed_roots',0) for x in (p/'runs').glob('*/progress.json'));status=f'{roots:,} / {n*count:,} roots; {successes}/{count} finished populations'
            elif not receipts and not any((p/'runs').glob('*/progress.json')):
                status='Queued; waiting for preceding workers'
            else:status=f'{len(receipts)}/{count} execution outcomes; {successes} successful exits'
            if done:status+='; fixed campaign finished'
            report=p/'REPORT.md';target=str(report if report.exists() else p/('design.json' if (p/'design.json').exists() else 'campaign.json'))
            purpose={'extended-scaling':'Declared prefixes 80k, 160k, 320k; no rare family discarded.','defensive-command-mixture/branching-comparison':'Same target and exact mixture correction; four seeds at each of 20k and 40k.','defensive-command-mixture/known-flight':'Later ACARS positions enter scoring only; one real flight is not a coverage study.','canonical-comparison':'Existing mode-stratified, observation-blocked implementation; same uncapped target.'}[folder]
            lines.append(f'| [{label}]({target}) | {status} | {purpose} |')
        ext=B/'extended-scaling/analysis/results.json'
        if ext.exists() and read(ext)['groups']:
            g=read(ext)['groups'][-1];lines+=['',f"Latest fully scored large-run prefix: **{g['budget']:,} roots per population**, maximum endpoint CDF gap **{100*g['worst_cdf_gap']:.3f} percentage points**, largest family **{100*g['largest_family']:.3f}%**. Later partial prefixes are not normalized or shown as complete."]
        candidate=B/'defensive-command-mixture/branching-comparison/analysis/results.json'
        if candidate.exists() and read(candidate)['groups']:
            g=read(candidate)['groups'][-1];lines+=['',f"Latest fully scored mixture budget: **{g['budget']:,} roots per population**, maximum endpoint CDF gap **{100*g['worst_cdf_gap']:.3f} points**, mean cost **{g['mean_cpu_seconds']:.1f} CPU seconds**. Read the full mode/evidence diagnostics before inferring an improvement."]
        lines+=['','Completed findings:','','- [Full-flight SMC comparison](defensive-command-mixture/recovery/REPORT.md): 48/48 runs completed; both methods still lose independent initial families and disagree substantially.','- [Independent conditional references](defensive-command-mixture/CONDITIONAL-REPORT.md): the command mixture improves two selected transition-integral estimates; that is insufficient to establish full-flight stability.','- [Pilot-calibrated branching recovery](calibrated-recovery/REPORT.md): 19/24 complete, five CPU-limited; up to 67 points of endpoint disagreement.','- [Accepted weather optimization](profiling/fused-weather/REPORT.md): identical trajectory bytes in paired controls, about 6% less CPU, integrated into canonical dynamics.','- [Original core benchmark](REPORT.md), [initial recovery controls](recovery/REPORT.md), and [separate endpoint/bridge review](../fuel-exhaustion-review/REVIEW.md).','','The larger populations continue to their fixed budgets regardless of intermediate results. Experimental proposals remain outside the product until supported by full comparisons.']
        p=B/'PROGRESS.md';tmp=p.with_suffix('.writing');tmp.write_text('\n'.join(lines)+'\n');tmp.replace(p);return complete
    if '--watch' in sys.argv:
        while True:
            if update():break
            time.sleep(30)
    else:update()

    from pathlib import Path
    p=Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/defensive-command-mixture/known-flight/analyze.py')
    s=p.read_text().replace('import csv,importlib.util,itertools,json,math','import csv,gzip,importlib.util,itertools,json,math\nfrom scipy.special import logsumexp')
    s=s.replace("    runs.append(m)", """    histories=[];history_log_weights=[]
        for path in sorted(out.glob('rows-*.jsonl.gz')):
            with gzip.open(path,'rt') as f:
                for line in f:
                    row=json.loads(line);histories.append(row['states']);history_log_weights.append(row['log_weight'])
        history=np.array(histories);history_weights=np.exp(np.array(history_log_weights)-logsumexp(history_log_weights))
        assert history.shape==(m['rows'],len(obs),8)
        assert np.allclose(history_weights,a['w'],atol=1e-14,rtol=1e-10)
        assert np.array_equal(history[:,-1,0],a['latitude']) and np.array_equal(history[:,-1,1],a['longitude'])
        checks=[]
        for k,observation in enumerate(obs):
            tr=truth[observation['epoch_id']];position=[float(tr['lat_deg']),float(tr['lon_deg'])]
            assert abs(float(tr['seconds_from_t0'])-float(observation['seconds_from_t0']))<1e-6
            lat=np.deg2rad(history[:,k,0]);lon=np.deg2rad(history[:,k,1]);la,lo=np.deg2rad(position)
            hav=np.sin((lat-la)/2)**2+np.cos(lat)*np.cos(la)*np.sin((lon-lo)/2)**2
            distance=2*(6371000/1852)*np.arcsin(np.sqrt(np.clip(hav,0,1)))
            checks.append(dict(epoch_id=observation['epoch_id'],seconds_from_t0=float(observation['seconds_from_t0']),truth_latitude_longitude=position,truth_cdf_ranks=[float(core.cdf(history[:,k,col],history_weights,[value])[0]) for col,value in enumerate(position)],truth_in_90_percent_marginal_intervals=[recovery.central_contains(history[:,k,col],history_weights,value,.9) for col,value in enumerate(position)],posterior_mean_distance_from_truth_nm=float(history_weights@distance)))
        assert np.allclose(checks[-1]['truth_cdf_ranks'],m['truth_cdf_ranks'])
        m['all_contact_smoothed_truth_checks']=checks
        m['all_contact_scope']='Earlier positions use final six-contact trajectory weights: smoothing, not real-time filtering. ACARS positions are scorer-only.'
        runs.append(m)""")
    p.write_text(s)

    """Restore frozen research sources only when reproducing this rejected experiment."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import hashlib,json,tarfile
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    record=json.loads((B/'rejected-checkout-manifest.json').read_text());target=Path(record['original_path']);archive=B/'rejected-checkout.tar.gz'
    sha=lambda data:hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    assert sha(archive.read_bytes())==record['archive_sha256']
    assert target.is_absolute() and target.parent==Path('/tmp') and target.name.startswith('mh370-defensive-command-')
    if not target.exists():
        with tarfile.open(archive) as source:
            members={m.name:m for m in source.getmembers()}
            assert set(members)=={r['path'] for r in record['files']}
            for row in record['files']:
                name=Path(row['path']);assert not name.is_absolute() and '..' not in name.parts
                member=members[row['path']];assert member.isfile()
                data=source.extractfile(member).read();assert len(data)==row['bytes'] and sha(data)==row['sha256']
                path=target/name;path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);path.write_bytes(data)
    for row in record['files']:
        path=target/row['path'];assert path.is_file() and sha(path.read_bytes())==row['sha256'],path
    print(f'All {len(record["files"])} research source identities verified at {target}. This is not product code.')

    """Existing branching engine, first-contact splitting restored; CPU-only sizing pilot."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import concurrent.futures,datetime,hashlib,json,math,os,resource,subprocess,sys,time
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    d=read(B/'design.json')
    if len(sys.argv)>1 and sys.argv[1]=='--measure':
        n=d['cpu_limit_seconds'];resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU,(n,n+10))
        os.execv(sys.executable,[sys.executable,str(B.parent/'measure.py'),*sys.argv[2:]])
    for p,h in d['identities'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    print('Waiting for the fixed canonical comparison to release its two workers.',flush=True)
    while not Path(d['resource_predecessor']).exists():time.sleep(2)

    def job(seed,roots,role):
        name=f'{role}-{roots}-seed-{seed}';path=B/'settings'/f'{name}.json'
        settings=read(B/'base-settings.json');settings.update(seed=seed,roots=roots)
        data=json.dumps(settings,indent=2)
        if path.exists():assert path.read_text()==data
        else:path.write_text(data)
        return dict(name=name,seed=seed,budget=roots,method='branching',tier=0,settings=str(path),settings_sha256=sha(path),role=role)
    def execute(j):
        out=B/'runs'/j['name'];receipt=out.with_suffix('.execution.json')
        if receipt.exists():return read(receipt)
        assert not out.exists(),f'Unrecorded partial output requires inspection: {out}'
        p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(B/'run.py'),'--measure',d['binary'],str(B/'model.json'),j['settings'],str(out)],capture_output=True,text=True)
        if not receipt.exists():raise RuntimeError(p.stdout+p.stderr)
        ex=read(receipt);zero='all particles have zero weight' in out.with_suffix('.log').read_text()
        if ex['returncode'] not in [0,-24] and not (ex['returncode']==1 and zero):raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected failure: {out}: {p.stdout} {p.stderr}')
        print(j['name'],ex['returncode'],ex['cpu_seconds'],flush=True);return ex
    pilots=[job(s,d['pilot_roots'],'cost-pilot') for s in d['pilot_seeds']]
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:outcomes=list(pool.map(execute,pilots))
    if any(x['returncode']"'!=0 for x in outcomes):
        (B/'"'completed.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='cost_pilot_failed_main_comparison_unavailable',outcomes=0,pilot_outcomes=outcomes),indent=2));raise SystemExit(0)
    mean_cpu=sum(x['cpu_seconds'] for x in outcomes)/len(outcomes)
    roots=max(2000,min(40000,1000*math.floor(d['target_cpu_seconds']/mean_cpu*d['pilot_roots']/1000)))
    jobs=[job(s,roots,'branching') for s in d['comparison_seeds']]
    campaign=dict(design_sha256=sha(B/'design.json'),sizing='Only process CPU from two fixed pilots is used; pilot posterior outputs are excluded.',pilot_jobs=pilots,pilot_execution_sha256={j['name']:sha((B/'runs'/j['name']).with_suffix('.execution.json')) for j in pilots},pilot_mean_cpu_seconds=mean_cpu,selected_roots=roots,jobs=jobs)
    data=json.dumps(campaign,indent=2);path=B/'campaign.json'
    if path.exists():assert path.read_text()==data
    else:path.write_text(data)
    print('Frozen main root budget:',roots,flush=True)
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:outcomes=list(pool.map(execute,jobs))
    (B/'completed.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='all_four_fixed_comparison_outcomes_retained',outcomes=len(outcomes),completed_utc=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),campaign_sha256=sha(path)),indent=2))

    from pathlib import Path
    import hashlib,json,datetime
    b=Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/all-contact-branching');parent=b.parent

    (b/'model.json').write_bytes((parent/'model.json').read_bytes())
    s=read(parent/'settings.json');s.update(start_root=0,roots=1000,batch_roots=1000,threads=1,skip_branch_after=[])
    assert s['branches']==3 and s['tau_refresh'] and s['corrected_guide']
    (b/'base-settings.json').write_text(json.dumps(s,indent=2))
    old=read(parent/'extended-scaling/campaign.json');ids={p:h for p,h in old['identities'].items() if '/extended-scaling/model.json' not in p}
    for p in [b/'model.json',b/'base-settings.json',b/'run.py',parent/'measure.py']:ids[str(p)]=sha(p)
    d=dict(created_utc=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),binary=old['binary'],workers=2,cpu_limit_seconds=1800,resource_predecessor=str(parent/'canonical-comparison/completed.json'),pilot_roots=1000,pilot_seeds=[20260925400,20260925401],comparison_seeds=list(range(20260925410,20260925414)),target_cpu_seconds=900,sizing_rule='Clamp floor(900 / mean pilot process CPU seconds * 1000 roots / 1000) * 1000 to [2000,40000]. Exact mode allocation is preserved. No posterior or pilot evidence enters sizing.',target='Same frozen uncapped cruise model, observations, original corrected guide, initial proposal, mode allocation and likelihood scales as core benchmark. No defensive command mixture.',change='skip_branch_after changes only from [0,1] to []; each nonterminal observation may branch into three children with weights divided by three. No physical equation, likelihood, proposal or executable change.',rationale='Recovered configuration propagates one early history per initial root through the first two contacts; an archived dominant family shared an influential near-18:28 turn. The earlier short-contact guide experiment changed proposal support and failed; this instead tests offspring placement. Davey Chapter 8 printed pages 56–57 describes parent-wise branching at resampling; this is not a claim to reproduce his unavailable exact data or tuning.',evaluation='Four fresh independent comparison seeds; all original core branching groups used as references at measured CPU. Reconcile every root, leaf and omitted mass; report all endpoint CDF gaps, family concentration, mode/evidence spread. CPU-capped and zero-support failures retained, never replaced. Cost pilots excluded from precision comparisons.',identities=ids)
    (b/'design.json').write_text(json.dumps(d,indent=2))
    for p,h in ids.items():assert sha(Path(p))==h,p
    print('Fixed source and settings verified; queued test changes only offspring placement.')

"'![Measured cost and independent-run precision](analysis/comparison.svg) | Offspring placement | Roots per run | Mean CPU (s) | Worst CDF gap (points) | Minimum family ESS | Largest family | Max mode spread (points) | Max/min evidence | |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:| '"'''+ '\n'.join(rows)+f''' The recovered configuration skips branching after the first two contacts. Here each nonterminal contact can produce three children, each inheriting one third of its parent's weight. This provides multiple continuations before the near-18:28 history is fixed. All likelihoods, physical priors, original guided-command corrections, initial proposal, mode allocation, roulette rule and known likelihood scales are unchanged. The executable includes only the previously verified exact weather optimization. The earlier short-contact guidance experiment changed a different part of the computation and failed; it is not relabelled as this test. Davey et al., *Bayesian Methods in the Search for MH370*, Chapter 8, printed pages 56–57, describe parent-wise branching and pruning as a way to explore large numbers of histories. This test addresses offspring placement only. It does not reconstruct unavailable published inputs or establish a complete Davey reproduction. See the [exact local book version](/jackbox/home/MH370/.sources/davey-2016-bayesian-search/paper/paper.pdf). Two separate 1,000-root cost pilots averaged **{c['pilot_mean_cpu_seconds']:.3f} process CPU seconds**. The predeclared CPU-only rule chose **{c['selected_roots']:,}** roots for each of four fresh comparison seeds. Pilot posterior outputs are excluded. Actual cost is reported rather than assumed to match the 900-second sizing target. Four seeds provide a development comparison, not a convergence certificate. Failed or capped runs are retained without replacements or partial-population normalization. ## Reproduction [Design and sizing rule](design.json), [derived fixed campaign](campaign.json), [results and identities](analysis/results.json), and [vector figure](analysis/comparison.pdf).       $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/all-contact-branching/analyze.py <<'PY'
    from pathlib import Path
    import importlib.util,json
    import numpy as np
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('core',B.parent/'analyze.py');core=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(core);core.B=B
    read=core.read;d=read(B/'design.json');(B/'analysis').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for p,h in d['identities'].items():assert core.sha(p)==h,p
    if not (B/'campaign.json').exists():
        print('Cost pilots have not frozen a main population size. No comparison posterior available.');raise SystemExit(0)
    c=read(B/'campaign.json');assert c['design_sha256']==core.sha(B/'design.json')
    runs=[];arrays=[];outcomes=[]
    for j in c['jobs']:
        out=B/'runs'/j['name'];receipt=out.with_suffix('.execution.json')
        if not receipt.exists():continue
        ex=read(receipt);outcomes.append(dict(name=j['name'],execution=ex))
        if ex['returncode']"'!=0:continue
        assert ex['"'identities'][d['binary']]==d['identities'][d['binary']]
        assert ex['identities'][j['settings']]==j['settings_sha256']
        if read(out/'completed.json')['terminal_rows']==0:continue
        a,m=core.compact(j);arrays.append(a);runs.append(m)
    groups=[]
    if len(runs)==4:
        pairs=core.compare(arrays,runs);modes=np.array([m['mode_mass'] for m in runs]);logz=np.array([m['log_evidence'] for m in runs]);variance={}
        for axis,grid in [('latitude',np.arange(-90,90.001,.25)),('longitude',np.arange(-180,180.001,.25))]:
            curves=np.array([core.cdf(a[axis],a['w'],grid) for a in arrays]);variance[axis]=dict(max_pointwise_sd=float(curves.std(axis=0,ddof=1).max()),integrated_cdf_variance_degree=float(curves.var(axis=0,ddof=1).sum()*.25))
        groups.append(dict(method='all_contact_branching',budget=c['selected_roots'],runs=4,worst_cdf_gap=max(p[k] for p in pairs for k in ['latitude_gap','longitude_gap']),max_joint_tv=max(p['joint_tv'] for p in pairs),minimum_root_ess=min(m['root_ess'] for m in runs),largest_family=max(m['largest_family'] for m in runs),evidence_ratio=float(np.exp(np.ptp(logz))),max_mode_spread=float(np.ptp(modes,axis=0).max()),mean_cpu_seconds=float(np.mean([m['execution']['cpu_seconds'] for m in runs])),variability=variance,pairwise=pairs))
    r=dict(scope='Four independent populations under the same physical target, changing only skip_branch_after from [0,1] to []. CPU-only pilot outputs are excluded from posterior comparisons.',campaign_sha256=core.sha(B/'campaign.json'),completed_outcomes=len(outcomes),runs=runs,groups=groups,outcomes=outcomes,reference_groups=[g for g in read(B.parent/'analysis/results.json')['groups'] if g['method']=='branching'])
    (B/'analysis/results.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print(json.dumps(dict(completed_outcomes=len(outcomes),groups=groups),indent=2))

    cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/all-contact-branching/report.py <<'PY'
    from pathlib import Path
    import json
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use('Agg')
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    r=json.loads((B/'analysis/results.json').read_text());c=json.loads((B/'campaign.json').read_text());assert r['completed_outcomes']==4
    plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':10,'pdf.fonttype':42})
    fig,axes=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(10,4.2),layout='constrained')
    for gg,label,color,marker in [(r['reference_groups'],'Skip first two contacts','#315b85','o'),(r['groups'],'Branch at every contact','#ad492d','s')]:
     if not gg:continue
     x=[g['mean_cpu_seconds'] for g in gg]
     axes[0].plot(x,[100*g['worst_cdf_gap'] for g in gg],marker+'-',lw=1,color=color,label=label)
     axes[1].plot(x,[g['minimum_root_ess'] for g in gg],marker+'-',lw=1,color=color,label=label)
    axes[0].set(xlabel='Mean process CPU seconds per population',ylabel='Maximum pairwise endpoint CDF gap\n(percentage points)',ylim=(0,100));axes[0].legend(frameon=False,fontsize=8)
    axes[1].set(xlabel='Mean process CPU seconds per population',ylabel='Minimum effective initial families',yscale='log')
    for ax in axes:ax.grid(alpha=.2)
    for ext in ['pdf','svg','png']:fig.savefig(B/'analysis'/f'comparison.{ext}',dpi=160)
    plt.close(fig)
    rows=[]
    for label,groups in [('Skip first two',r['reference_groups']),('All contacts',r['groups'])]:
     for g in groups:rows.append(f"| {label} | {g['budget']:,} | {g['mean_cpu_seconds']:.1f} | {100*g['worst_cdf_gap']:.2f} | {g['minimum_root_ess']:.2f} | {100*g['largest_family']:.2f}% | {100*g['max_mode_spread']:.2f} | {g['evidence_ratio']:.3f} |")
    summary='; '.join(f"{g['budget']:,} roots per run: worst endpoint gap {100*g['worst_cdf_gap']:.2f} points, minimum family ESS {g['minimum_root_ess']:.2f}" for g in r['groups']) or 'Four complete posteriors are unavailable; no complete-group precision estimate.'
    (B/'REPORT.md').write_text(f'''# Branching at every observation contact

    All four fixed comparison outcomes are retained. **{summary}**. This is a configuration experiment with the existing executable, not a new physical model or proposal.

    "'![Measured cost and independent-run precision](analysis/comparison.svg)

    | Offspring placement | Roots per run | Mean CPU (s) | Worst CDF gap (points) | Minimum family ESS | Largest family | Max mode spread (points) | Max/min evidence |
    |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
    '"'''+ '\n'.join(rows)+f'''

    The recovered configuration skips branching after the first two contacts. Here each nonterminal contact can produce three children, each inheriting one third of its parent's weight. This provides multiple continuations before the near-18:28 history is fixed. All likelihoods, physical priors, original guided-command corrections, initial proposal, mode allocation, roulette rule and known likelihood scales are unchanged. The executable includes only the previously verified exact weather optimization. The earlier short-contact guidance experiment changed a different part of the computation and failed; it is not relabelled as this test.

    Davey et al., *Bayesian Methods in the Search for MH370*, Chapter 8, printed pages 56–57, describe parent-wise branching and pruning as a way to explore large numbers of histories. This test addresses offspring placement only. It does not reconstruct unavailable published inputs or establish a complete Davey reproduction. See the [exact local book version](/jackbox/home/MH370/.sources/davey-2016-bayesian-search/paper/paper.pdf).

    Two separate 1,000-root cost pilots averaged **{c['pilot_mean_cpu_seconds']:.3f} process CPU seconds**. The predeclared CPU-only rule chose **{c['selected_roots']:,}** roots for each of four fresh comparison seeds. Pilot posterior outputs are excluded. Actual cost is reported rather than assumed to match the 900-second sizing target. Four seeds provide a development comparison, not a convergence certificate. Failed or capped runs are retained without replacements or partial-population normalization.

    ## Reproduction

    [Design and sizing rule](design.json), [derived fixed campaign](campaign.json), [results and identities](analysis/results.json), and [vector figure](analysis/comparison.pdf).

    "'```bash
    python {B}/run.py

    OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 /jackbox/home/MH370/.venv/bin/python {B}/report.py
    ```

    The runner waits for the canonical comparison'"'s workers and refuses unrecorded partial output. Completed receipts are reused. All four main seeds and all failures remain in the result.
    ''');print(summary)

    """Run declared analyses after fixed populations finish; never decide convergence."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import concurrent.futures,json,os,subprocess,time
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    PYTHON='/jackbox/home/MH370/.venv/bin/python'
    env=dict(os.environ,OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS='1',OMP_NUM_THREADS='1')
    def finish(folder,scripts):
        path=B/folder
        while not (path/'completed.json').exists():time.sleep(10)
        if folder=='all-contact-branching' and not (path/'campaign.json').exists():
            print(folder,'Cost pilot failure: main posteriors unavailable.',flush=True);return
        for script in scripts:
            destination=path/(script.removesuffix('.py')+'-completion.log')
            with destination.open('w') as output:
                result=subprocess.run([PYTHON,str(path/script)],env=env,stdout=output,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            if result.returncode:raise RuntimeError(f'{folder}/{script}: failed; inspect {destination}')
            print(folder,script,'completed',flush=True)
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        jobs=[pool.submit(finish,'extended-scaling',['analyze.py','report.py','uncertainty.py']),pool.submit(finish,'all-contact-branching',['analyze.py','report.py'])]
        for job in jobs:job.result()
    print('Both fixed comparisons scored; figures and scientific conclusions still require review.',flush=True)

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/aggregate-reference/analyze_extension.py <<'PY'
    """Add the declared four full populations to fixed earlier composite groups."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import contextlib,hashlib,importlib.util,io,itertools,json,math
    import numpy as np
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    d=read(B/'extension-design.json')
    for p,h in d['identities'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    extended=B.parent/'extended-scaling';c=read(extended/'campaign.json')
    scored=read(extended/'analysis/results.json');assert any(g['budget']==320000 and g['runs']==4 for g in scored['groups'])
    # Reuse the existing, checked unnormalized-weight calculation; its baseline
    # result is deterministic and must remain byte-identical to the frozen identity.
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('baseline_pool',B/'analyze.py');base=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):spec.loader.exec_module(base)
    assert sha(B/'results.json')==d['identities'][str(B/'results.json')]
    old=list(base.datasets)
    for assignment in d['assignments']:
        seed=assignment['new_seed'];lane=assignment['composite_group_index'];j=next(j for j in c['jobs'] if j['seed']==seed)
        assert base.sha(j['settings'])==j['settings_sha256']
        settings=read(j['settings']);original=read(B.parent/'settings.json')
        for key in ['branches','log_eta','peak_scale','extra_log_scale','skip_branch_after','tau_refresh','corrected_guide','roulette_mode','initial_proposal','mode_allocation']:assert settings[key]==original[key],key
        assert read(extended/'runs'/j['name']/'model.json')==base.target
        path=extended/'analysis'/f'seed-{seed}-roots-320000.npz';meta=read(path.with_suffix('.json'));assert meta['execution']['returncode']==0 and meta['budget']==320000
        assert sorted(p['seed'] for p in old if p['lane']==lane)==assignment['old_seeds']
        with np.load(path,allow_pickle=False) as z:a={k:z[k] for k in z.files}
        with np.errstate(divide='ignore'):logu=np.log(a['w'])+math.log(meta['budget'])+meta['log_evidence']
        base.add(seed,320000,lane,'extended-scaling',path,a,logu,a['root'],a['mode'],meta['log_evidence'],meta['omitted_mass_fraction'])
        base.files[str(path.with_suffix('.json'))]=sha(path.with_suffix('.json'))
    lanes=[base.pool([p for p in base.datasets if p['lane']==i]) for i in range(4)]
    combined,metrics=base.pool(base.datasets);pairs=[dict(a=i,b=j,**base.gap(lanes[i][0],lanes[j][0])) for i,j in itertools.combinations(range(4),2)]
    focal_share=float(combined['w'][combined['root']==base.focal_id].sum())
    old_pool,_=base.pool(old);new_pool,new_metrics=base.pool([p for p in base.datasets if p['cohort']=='extended-scaling'])
    old_checks=[base.pool([p for p in old if p['lane']==i])[1] for i in range(4)]
    for before,recorded in zip(old_checks,base.result['balanced_groups']):
        for key in ['root_ess','largest_family','log_evidence']:assert abs(before[key]-recorded[key])<1e-9
    result=dict(scope='Earlier retrospective groups, each extended by one prospectively assigned fresh 320,000-root population. Same streams are nested between old and augmented comparisons; these are not four new 690,000-root simulations. No outlier removed.',design_sha256=sha(B/'extension-design.json'),baseline=base.result['combined'],baseline_largest_balanced_cdf_gap=base.result['largest_balanced_cdf_gap'],baseline_focal_root_mass=base.result['focal_root']['pooled_mass'],combined=metrics,balanced_groups=[m for _,m in lanes],balanced_pairwise=pairs,largest_balanced_cdf_gap=max(p[k] for p in pairs for k in ['latitude','longitude']),old_pool_vs_new_pool=base.gap(old_pool,new_pool),new_pool=new_metrics,focal_root=dict(seed=2026092202,root=78645,pooled_mass=focal_share),source_qualification=base.result['qualification'],input_sha256=base.files)
    (B/'extension-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps({k:result[k] for k in ['combined','largest_balanced_cdf_gap','old_pool_vs_new_pool','focal_root']},indent=2))
    PY

    from pathlib import Path

    from pathlib import Path

    if (B/'extension-results.json').exists():
     e=json.loads((B/'extension-results.json').read_text());ec=e['combined']
     leading=f\"The prospectively assigned extension now pools **{ec['roots']:,} roots across 24 seeds**. Its four augmented composite groups disagree by up to **{100*e['largest_balanced_cdf_gap']:.2f} percentage points**. The previously dominant family remains included, carrying **{100*e['focal_root']['pooled_mass']:.2f}%** of the full pool. This is a retrospective pooling diagnostic, not an additional independent replication campaign.\\n\\n\"
     erows='\\n'.join(f\"| {i+1} | {g['roots']:,} | {g['root_ess']:.1f} | {100*g['largest_family']:.2f}% | {g['log_evidence']:.6f} |\" for i,g in enumerate(e['balanced_groups']))
     extension_section=f'''## Extension retaining the historical rare family

    Each original composite receives the full new 320,000-root population assigned by seed index before the complete new populations were scored. The old and extended comparisons share earlier samples and are not independent extra replications. No rare family, failed old finding or unfavorable seed is discarded. All pooling uses unnormalized mass and original root denominators.

    | Augmented composite | Roots | Family ESS | Largest family | Log evidence |
    |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
    {erows}

    The full extended pool has family ESS **{ec['root_ess']:.1f}**, largest family **{100*ec['largest_family']:.2f}%**, and log evidence **{ec['log_evidence']:.6f}**. The original 1.48-million-root pool and new 1.28-million-root pool have latitude/longitude CDF gaps **{100*e['old_pool_vs_new_pool']['latitude']:.2f}/{100*e['old_pool_vs_new_pool']['longitude']:.2f} points**. Their agreement is a numerical diagnostic, not comparison with a known posterior.

      $ python - <<'PY'
    from pathlib import Path
    import json,hashlib
    base=Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling')
    def sha(p):
     h=hashlib.sha256()
     with p.open('rb') as f:
      for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(1<<20),b''):h.update(chunk)
     return h.hexdigest()
    for name,design_name in [('extended-scaling','campaign.json'),('aggregate-reference','extension-design.json')]:
     b=base/name;d=json.loads((b/design_name).read_text())
     for path,digest in d['identities'].items():assert sha(Path(path))==digest,path
     if name=='extended-scaling':assert json.loads((b/'analysis/results.json').read_text())['groups'][-1]['budget']==320000
     else:assert json.loads((b/'extension-results.json').read_text())['combined']['roots']==2760000
     files=[dict(path=str(p.relative_to(b)),bytes=p.stat().st_size,sha256=sha(p)) for p in sorted(b.rglob('*')) if p.is_file() and p.name"'!='"'artifact-manifest.json' and '__pycache__' not in p.parts]
     (b/'artifact-manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='Fixed full populations or specified retrospective extension complete, scored and reviewed. Numerical improvement does not certify unseen-family absence or physical calibration.',design_sha256=sha(b/design_name),external_identities=d['identities'],files=files),indent=2))
     print(name,len(files),sum(f['bytes'] for f in files),flush=True)

    cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/known-flight-scaling/run.py <<'PY'
    """Fixed nested known-flight scaling; same four original seeds and pilot scales."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import concurrent.futures,datetime,hashlib,json,os,resource,subprocess,sys
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    d=read(B/'design.json')
    if len(sys.argv)>1 and sys.argv[1]=='--measure':
        n=d['cpu_limit_seconds'];resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU,(n,n+10))
        os.execv(sys.executable,[sys.executable,str(B.parent/'measure.py'),*sys.argv[2:]])
    for p,h in d['identities'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    for j in d['jobs']:assert sha(j['settings'])==j['settings_sha256']
    def run(j):
        out=B/'runs'/j['name'];receipt=out.with_suffix('.execution.json')
        if receipt.exists():return read(receipt)
        assert not out.exists(),f'Unrecorded partial output requires inspection: {out}'
        p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(B/'run.py'),'--measure',d['branching_binary'],str(B/'model.json'),j['settings'],str(out)],capture_output=True,text=True)
        if not receipt.exists():raise RuntimeError(p.stdout+p.stderr)
        ex=read(receipt);zero='all particles have zero weight' in out.with_suffix('.log').read_text()
        if ex['returncode'] not in [0,-24] and not (ex['returncode']==1 and zero):raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected failure {out}: {p.stdout} {p.stderr}')
        print(j['name'],ex['returncode'],ex['cpu_seconds'],flush=True);return ex
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=d['workers']) as pool:outcomes=list(pool.map(run,d['jobs']))
    (B/'completed.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='all_four_fixed_outcomes_retained',outcomes=len(outcomes),design_sha256=sha(B/'design.json'),completed_utc=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()),indent=2))
    PY

Old first 8,000 roots must replay exactly for each seed before interpreting nested scaling.',evaluation='Scorer-only ACARS at all six contacts, final and smoothed earlier positions; all pairwise endpoint CDF gaps, root concentration, mode/evidence spread. No accident-trained initial proposal, fitted truth bias, fuel or new physical equation. Main evidence scales inherited from the independent original pilot, not learned from these outputs.',identities={str(p):sha(p) for p in paths})
    (b/'design.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2));(b/'derived-settings.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(jobs=jobs),indent=2))
    for p,h in record['identities'].items():assert sha(Path(p))==h,p
    print('Four fixed nested 128k populations verified. No proposal or physical-model change.')

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/known-flight-scaling/verify_prefixes.py <<'PY'
    """Check exact reuse of every old 8,000-root population before scoring extensions."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import gzip,hashlib,json,time
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text());sha=lambda data:hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    d=read(B/'design.json');checks=[]
    for j in d['jobs']:
     old=Path(d['baseline'])/'runs'/j['baseline_name'];new=B/'runs'/j['name']
     original=read(Path(j['baseline_settings']));larger=read(Path(j['settings']))
     assert original==dict(larger,roots=8000)
     rootfiles=sorted(old.glob('roots-*.json'));assert len(rootfiles)==16
     for src in rootfiles:
      dst=new/src.name;rows=src.name.replace('roots-','rows-').replace('.json','.jsonl.gz');old_rows=old/rows;new_rows=new/rows
      while not dst.exists() or not new_rows.exists():
       if new.with_suffix('.execution.json').exists():raise RuntimeError(f'Execution ended before reference prefix was available: {new}')
       time.sleep(2)
      # A batch can be observed before its compressed writer closes. Only inspect
      # it once the independently written progress record includes its end root.
      end=int(src.stem.split('-')[-1])
      while not (new/'progress.json').exists() or read(new/'progress.json')['completed_roots']<end:time.sleep(2)
      assert src.read_bytes()==dst.read_bytes(),dst
      before=gzip.decompress(old_rows.read_bytes());after=gzip.decompress(new_rows.read_bytes());assert before==after,new_rows
      checks.append(dict(seed=j['seed'],roots_file=src.name,root_records_sha256=sha(src.read_bytes()),terminal_rows_file=rows,decompressed_terminal_sha256=sha(before)))
     print(j['seed'],'all original 8,000 roots and terminal rows replay exactly',flush=True)
    (B/'prefix-replay.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='All four original 8,000-root populations replay byte for byte under the larger requested root count; only the requested count differs.',design_sha256=sha((B/'design.json').read_bytes()),checks=checks),indent=2))

      use std::collections::BTreeMap;

      use rand::Rng;
      use rayon::prelude::*;
      use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
      use thiserror::Error;

      use crate::{
          aggregate_log_weights_by_ancestor, effective_sample_size, normalize_log_weights, rng_for,

      };

      #[derive(Debug, Error)]
      pub enum SmcError {
          #[error("invalid filter configuration: {0}")]
          InvalidConfiguration(&'static str),
          #[error("observation times must be finite, ordered, and not precede the initial time")]
          InvalidObservationTime,
          #[error("particle model failed: {0}")]
          Model(String),
          #[error("particle weight is NaN or positive infinity")]
          InvalidWeights,
          #[error("all particles received zero probability")]
          AllParticlesRejected,
          #[error("systematic-resampling offset is outside [0, 1/N)")]
          InvalidResamplingOffset,
          #[error("particle ancestry is absent or internally inconsistent")]
          InvalidAncestry,
          #[error("requested filter snapshot does not exist")]
          InvalidSnapshot,
          #[error("particle state does not carry its declared immutable stratum")]
          StratumMismatch,
          #[error("all particles in stratum {0:?} received zero probability")]
          StratumRejected(StratumId),
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct StratumDiagnostics {
          pub id: StratumId,
          pub particle_count: usize,
          pub posterior_mass: f64,
          /// Log mass remains informative when the corresponding linear mass
          /// underflows to zero.
          pub log_posterior_mass: Option<f64>,
          /// ESS after weights are normalized within this stratum.
          pub conditional_ess: f64,
          pub maximum_normalized_weight: f64,
          pub maximum_normalized_log_weight: Option<f64>,
          pub distinct_root_ancestors: usize,
          pub root_effective_sample_size: f64,
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct FilterCheckpoint {
          pub observation_index: usize,
          pub observation_time_s: f64,
          pub guide_ess: Option<f64>,
          pub posterior_ess: f64,
          pub ancestor_resampled: bool,
          pub posterior_resampled: bool,
          pub log_evidence_increment: f64,
          pub cumulative_log_evidence: f64,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub maximum_normalized_weight: f64,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub distinct_root_ancestors: usize,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub root_effective_sample_size: f64,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub maximum_root_weight: f64,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_6: usize,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_3: usize,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub strata: Vec<StratumDiagnostics>,
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct FilterSnapshot<S> {
          pub observation_index: Option<usize>,
          pub observation_time_s: f64,
          pub particles: Vec<S>,
          pub log_weights: Vec<f64>,
          pub root_ids: Vec<usize>,
          #[serde(default)]
          pub stratum_ids: Vec<StratumId>,
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct FilterParticleIdentity {
          /// `None` identifies the initialized population; `Some(i)` identifies the
          /// population immediately after assimilating observation `i`.
          pub observation_index: Option<usize>,
          pub particle_index: usize,
      }

      impl<S> FilterSnapshot<S> {
          pub fn particle_identity(
              &self,
              particle_index: usize,
          ) -> Result<FilterParticleIdentity, SmcError> {
              if particle_index >= self.particles.len() {
                  return Err(SmcError::InvalidSnapshot);
              }
              Ok(FilterParticleIdentity {
                  observation_index: self.observation_index,
                  particle_index,
              })
          }

    //"'! Independent depth-first root trees with unbiased splitting and roulette.
    //!
    //! Terminal callbacks receive computationally scaled log weights. Subtract the
    //! sum of configured likelihood scales exactly once when estimating evidence;
    //! normalized posterior weights are invariant to that common scale.
    use rand::Rng;
    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

    use crate::{logsumexp, rng_for, ParticleModel, SmcError, StratumId};

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    pub struct BranchingConfig {
        pub seed: u64,
        pub initial_time_s: f64,
        pub children: usize,
        pub log_roulette_threshold: f64,
        pub log_likelihood_scales: Vec<f64>,
        /// Contacts at which the weighted state continues once, without roulette.
        pub skip_branch_after: Vec<usize>,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
    pub struct BranchingRootSpec {
        pub index: usize,
        pub stratum: StratumId,
        pub index_within_stratum: usize,
        pub stratum_population: usize,
        /// log(scientific stratum probability / computational allocation fraction).
        pub log_stratum_correction: f64,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    pub struct BranchingStage {
        pub attempts: u64,
        pub supported: u64,
        pub branched: u64,
        pub roulette_survivors: u64,
        pub pruned: u64,
        pub log_mass: f64,
        pub log_square_mass: f64,
        pub max_log_weight: f64,
    }
    impl Default for BranchingStage {
        fn default() -> Self {
            Self {
                attempts: 0, supported: 0, branched: 0, roulette_survivors: 0, pruned: 0,
                log_mass: f64::NEG_INFINITY, log_square_mass: f64::NEG_INFINITY,
                max_log_weight: f64::NEG_INFINITY,
            }
        }
    }

    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
    pub struct BranchingRootResult<S> {
        pub initial_state: S,
        pub stages: Vec<BranchingStage>,
        pub terminal_states: usize,
    }

    fn log_add(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64 {
        if a == f64::NEG_INFINITY { b }
        else if b == f64::NEG_INFINITY { a }
        else { let m = a.max(b); m + ((a-m).exp() + (b-m).exp()).ln() }
    }
    fn valid_weight(value: f64) -> Result<(), SmcError> {
        if value.is_nan() || value == f64::INFINITY { Err(SmcError::InvalidWeights) }
        else { Ok(()) }
    }

    /// Validate once before launching any independent root jobs.
    pub fn validate_branching<M: ParticleModel>(
        model: &M, observations: &[M::Observation], config: &BranchingConfig,
    ) -> Result<(), SmcError> {
        if observations.is_empty() || config.children == 0
            || !config.initial_time_s.is_finite() || !config.log_roulette_threshold.is_finite()
            || config.log_likelihood_scales.len() != observations.len()
            || config.log_likelihood_scales.iter().any(|x| !x.is_finite())
            || config.skip_branch_after.iter().any(|&i| i >= observations.len()-1)
        { return Err(SmcError::InvalidConfiguration("invalid branching configuration")); }
        let mut previous = config.initial_time_s;
        for observation in observations {
            let time = model.observation_time_s(observation);
            if !time.is_finite() || time < previous { return Err(SmcError::InvalidObservationTime); }
            previous = time;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Walk one independently keyed root. The callback sees every finite terminal
    /// weight before any output compression; errors abort the root, never yield a
    /// completed partial tree. The history contains states after each observation.
    /// Call `validate_branching` before scheduling a population.
    pub fn run_branching_root<M, F>(
        model: &M, observations: &[M::Observation], config: &BranchingConfig,
        root: BranchingRootSpec, mut terminal: F,
    ) -> Result<BranchingRootResult<M::State>, SmcError>
    where M: ParticleModel,
          F: FnMut(usize, &M::State, f64, &[usize], &[M::State]) -> Result<(), SmcError>,
    {
        if observations.is_empty() || config.log_likelihood_scales.len() != observations.len()
            || config.children == 0 || root.stratum_population == 0
            || root.index_within_stratum >= root.stratum_population
            || !root.log_stratum_correction.is_finite()
        { return Err(SmcError::InvalidConfiguration("invalid independent root")); }
        // Retain the stream identity of the validated recovered implementation.
        let mut rng = rng_for(config.seed, "davey-depth-first-root-v2", 0, root.index, 0);
        let initial = model.initialize_in_stratum(root.stratum, root.index,
            root.index_within_stratum, root.stratum_population, &mut rng)
            .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
        if model.stratum(&initial.state) != root.stratum { return Err(SmcError::StratumMismatch); }
        let initial_weight = initial.log_prior_over_proposal + root.log_stratum_correction;
        valid_weight(initial_weight)?;
        let mut result = BranchingRootResult { initial_state: initial.state.clone(),
            stages: vec![BranchingStage::default(); observations.len()], terminal_states: 0 };
        struct Walker<'"'a, M: ParticleModel, F> {
            model: &'a M, observations: &'a [M::Observation], config: &'a BranchingConfig,
            stratum: StratumId, rng: &'a mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng, terminal: &'a mut F,
            result: &'a mut BranchingRootResult<M::State>,
        }
        impl<M, F> Walker<'_, M, F>
        where M: ParticleModel,
              F: FnMut(usize, &M::State, f64, &[usize], &[M::State]) -> Result<(), SmcError>,
        {
            fn visit(&mut self, state: &M::State, k: usize, weight: f64,
                path: &mut Vec<usize>, history: &mut Vec<M::State>) -> Result<(), SmcError> {
                self.result.stages[k].attempts += 1;
                let time = self.model.observation_time_s(&self.observations[k]);
                let previous = if k == 0 { self.config.initial_time_s }
                    else { self.model.observation_time_s(&self.observations[k-1]) };
                let proposal = self.model.propose(state, &self.observations[k], time-previous, self.rng)
                    .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
                let mut next = proposal.state;
                if self.model.stratum(&next) "'!= self.stratum { return Err(SmcError::StratumMismatch); }
                let likelihood = self.model.observe(&mut next, &self.observations[k])
                    .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
                let next_weight = weight + likelihood + proposal.log_prior_over_proposal
                    + self.config.log_likelihood_scales[k];
                valid_weight(next_weight)?;
                if next_weight == f64::NEG_INFINITY {
                    self.result.stages[k].pruned += 1; return Ok(());
                }
                let stage = &mut self.result.stages[k];
                stage.supported += 1;
                stage.log_mass = log_add(stage.log_mass, next_weight);
                stage.log_square_mass = log_add(stage.log_square_mass, 2.*next_weight);
                stage.max_log_weight = stage.max_log_weight.max(next_weight);
                history.push(next.clone());
                if k+1 == self.observations.len() {
                    (self.terminal)(self.result.terminal_states, &next, next_weight, path, history)?;
                    self.result.terminal_states += 1;
                } else if self.config.skip_branch_after.contains(&k) {
                    path.push(0); self.visit(&next, k+1, next_weight, path, history)?; path.pop();
                } else {
                    let (count, child_weight) = if next_weight >= self.config.log_roulette_threshold {
                        self.result.stages[k].branched += 1;
                        (self.config.children, next_weight - (self.config.children as f64).ln())
                    } else if self.rng.gen::<f64>().ln() < next_weight-self.config.log_roulette_threshold {
                        self.result.stages[k].roulette_survivors += 1;
                        (1, self.config.log_roulette_threshold)
                    } else {
                        self.result.stages[k].pruned += 1; (0, 0.)
                    };
                    for child in 0..count {
                        path.push(child); self.visit(&next, k+1, child_weight, path, history)?; path.pop();
                    }
                }
                history.pop(); Ok(())
            }
        }
        Walker { model, observations, config, stratum: root.stratum, rng: &mut rng,
            terminal: &mut terminal, result: &mut result }
            .visit(&initial.state, 0, initial_weight, &mut Vec::new(), &mut Vec::new())?;
        Ok(result)
    }

    /// Root masses include zero-mass trees and computational likelihood scales.
    /// A complete fixed population, including every declared zero root, is required.
    pub fn branching_log_evidence(root_log_masses: &[f64], total_log_scale: f64) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {
        if root_log_masses.is_empty() || !total_log_scale.is_finite() {
            return Err(SmcError::InvalidConfiguration("invalid branching evidence inputs"));
        }
        for &weight in root_log_masses { valid_weight(weight)?; }
        Ok(logsumexp(root_log_masses)? - (root_log_masses.len() as f64).ln() - total_log_scale)
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    mod tests {
        use super::*;
        use crate::{Initialization, Proposal};
        use std::convert::Infallible;
        #[derive(Clone)]
        struct State { mode: usize, bit: usize }
        struct FiniteModel { invalid: Option<f64> }
        impl ParticleModel for FiniteModel {
            type State = State;
            type Observation = usize;
            type Error = Infallible;
            fn observation_time_s(&self, k: &usize) -> f64 { *k as f64 + 1. }
            fn initialize(&self, _: usize, _: &mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<State, Infallible> { unreachable"'!() }
            fn initialize_in_stratum(&self, mode: StratumId, _: usize, bit: usize, _: usize,
                _: &mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<Initialization<State>, Infallible> {
                let zero_probability = [0.2_f64, 0.6][mode.0 as usize];
                let prior = if bit == 0 { zero_probability } else { 1.-zero_probability };
                Ok(Initialization { state: State { mode: mode.0 as usize, bit },
                    log_prior_over_proposal: (prior/0.5).ln() })
            }
            fn stratum(&self, state: &State) -> StratumId { StratumId(state.mode as u32) }
            fn propose(&self, state: &State, _: &usize, _: f64, _: &mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng)
                -> Result<Proposal<State>, Infallible> { Ok(Proposal::from_prior(state.clone())) }
            fn log_likelihood(&self, state: &State, k: &usize) -> Result<f64, Infallible> {
                if let Some(invalid) = self.invalid { return Ok(invalid); }
                let probabilities = [[[0.4_f64, 0.5], [0.6, 0.75]], [[0.2, 0.8], [0.5, 0.3]]];
                Ok(probabilities[state.mode][state.bit][*k].ln())
            }
        }
        fn config() -> BranchingConfig {
            BranchingConfig { seed: 3, initial_time_s: 0., children: 3,
                log_roulette_threshold: -100., log_likelihood_scales: vec![4.,5.], skip_branch_after: vec![] }
        }
        fn root(mode: usize, bit: usize) -> BranchingRootSpec {
            BranchingRootSpec { index: mode*2+bit, stratum: StratumId(mode as u32),
                index_within_stratum: bit, stratum_population: 2,
                log_stratum_correction: ([0.25_f64,0.75][mode]/0.5).ln() }
        }
        #[test]
        fn finite_prior_proposal_and_splitting_match_independent_evidence() {
            let model = FiniteModel { invalid: None }; let cfg = config();
            let mut masses = vec![]; let mut leaves = vec![];
            for mode in 0..2 { for bit in 0..2 {
                let result = run_branching_root(&model, &[0,1], &cfg, root(mode,bit),
                    |_, state, weight, path, history| {
                        assert_eq!(path.len(),1); assert_eq!(history.len(),2);
                        leaves.push((state.mode,weight)); Ok(())
                    }).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(result.terminal_states,3);
                masses.push(result.stages[1].log_mass);
            }}
            // Independently: 0.25*(0.2*0.4*0.5+0.8*0.6*0.75)
            //              +0.75*(0.6*0.2*0.8+0.4*0.5*0.3) = 0.217.
            let z = branching_log_evidence(&masses,9.).unwrap().exp();
            assert!((z-0.217).abs()<1e-14);
            let mode_one: f64 = leaves.iter().filter(|(mode,_)| *mode==1).map(|(_,w)|(w-9.).exp()/4.).sum();
            assert!((mode_one/z-0.117/0.217).abs()<1e-14);
            // Omitting the four-root denominator would give a fourfold error.
            assert!((logsumexp(&masses).unwrap()-9.).exp()>3.9*z);
        }
        #[test]
        fn roulette_preserves_mass_at_a_nonunit_threshold() {
            // Eight equally probable uniforms integrate p=1/8 exactly. A surviving
            // weight of 2 therefore represents the original mass 1/4, not 1/8.
            let represented: f64 = (0..8).map(|i| {
                roulette(0.25_f64.ln(),2_f64.ln(),((i as f64+0.5)/8.).ln())
                    .map_or(0.,f64::exp)/8.
            }).sum();
            assert!((represented-0.25).abs()<1e-15);
            assert!(roulette(-800.,0.,0.5_f64.ln()).is_none());
        }
        #[test]
        fn zero_support_is_distinguished_from_invalid_weights_and_failed_output() {
            let cfg = config();
            let zero = run_branching_root(&FiniteModel { invalid: Some(f64::NEG_INFINITY) }, &[0,1], &cfg,
                root(0,0), |_,_,_,_,_| panic!("zero mass must not be emitted")).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(zero.terminal_states,0);
            assert!(matches!(branching_log_evidence(&[f64::NEG_INFINITY],9.),Err(SmcError::AllParticlesRejected)));
            assert!(matches!(run_branching_root(&FiniteModel { invalid: Some(f64::NAN) }, &[0,1], &cfg,
                root(0,0), |_,_,_,_,_| Ok(())),Err(SmcError::InvalidWeights)));
            assert!(matches!(run_branching_root(&FiniteModel { invalid: None }, &[0,1], &cfg,
                root(0,0), |_,_,_,_,_| Err(SmcError::Model("output failed".into()))),Err(SmcError::Model(_))));
        }
    }

    import json
    import numpy as np
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use('Agg')
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
    d=read(B/'design.json');r=read(B/'analysis/results.json');old=read(Path(d['baseline'])/'analysis/results.json');assert r['completed_execution_outcomes']==4

    plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':10,'pdf.fonttype':42})
    fig,axes=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(10,7),layout='constrained');bounds=[[t] for t in target]

     for m in runs:
      with np.load(folder/'analysis'/f"{m['name']}.npz",allow_pickle=False) as a:
       for col,axis in enumerate(['latitude','longitude']):

    from pathlib import Path
    p=Path('/tmp/mh370-independent-branching-f3bdfhp5/source/crates/particle-filter/src/branching.rs');s=p.read_text();s=s.rsplit('}',1)[0]+r'''
        struct BiasedHmm;
        impl ParticleModel for BiasedHmm {
            type State = State; type Observation = usize; type Error = Infallible;
            fn observation_time_s(&self, k: &usize) -> f64 { *k as f64+1. }
            fn initialize(&self, _:usize, _: &mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<State,Infallible> { unreachable"'!() }
            fn initialize_in_stratum(&self, _:StratumId, _:usize, _:usize, _:usize, rng:&mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<Initialization<State>,Infallible> {
                let one = rng.gen_bool(0.6);
                Ok(Initialization { state: State { mode:0,bit:usize::from(one) },
                    log_prior_over_proposal: if one { (0.3_f64/0.6).ln() } else { (0.7_f64/0.4).ln() } })
            }
            fn propose(&self, state:&State, _: &usize, _:f64, rng:&mut rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<Proposal<State>,Infallible> {
                let flip = rng.gen_bool(0.5);
                Ok(Proposal { state: State { mode:0,bit:state.bit''^usize::from(flip) },
                    log_prior_over_proposal: if flip { (0.2_f64/0.5).ln() } else { (0.8_f64/0.5).ln() } })
            }
            fn log_likelihood(&self, state:&State, k:&usize) -> Result<f64,Infallible> {
                Ok([[0.15_f64,0.8],[0.7,0.2],[0.1,0.9]][*k][state.bit].ln())
            }
        }
        #[test]
        fn biased_hidden_markov_proposals_recover_independently_enumerated_joint() {
            // Enumerate every target history, independently of the proposal and tree.
            let likelihood=[[0.15_f64,0.8],[0.7,0.2],[0.1,0.9]]; let mut exact=[0.;2];
            for mask in 0..16 {
                let x:Vec<usize>=(0..4).map(|k|(mask>>k)&1).collect();
                let mut mass=if x[0]==1 {0.3} else {0.7};
                for k in 0..3 { mass*=if x[k+1]==x[k] {0.8} else {0.2}; mass*=likelihood[k][x[k+1]]; }
                exact[x[3]]+=mass;
            }
            let n=100_000; let log_scale=4_f64.ln();
            let cfg=BranchingConfig { seed:91, initial_time_s:0.,children:3,
                log_roulette_threshold:0.,log_likelihood_scales:vec![log_scale;3],skip_branch_after:vec![] };
            let mut sum=[0.;2];let mut squares=[0.;2];
            for index in 0..n {
                let spec=BranchingRootSpec { index,stratum:StratumId(0),index_within_stratum:index,
                    stratum_population:n,log_stratum_correction:0. };
                let mut joint=[0.;2];
                run_branching_root(&BiasedHmm,&[0,1,2],&cfg,spec,|_,state,weight,_,_,_| {
                    joint[state.bit]+=(weight-3.*log_scale).exp();Ok(())
                }).unwrap();
                for k in 0..2 {sum[k]+=joint[k];squares[k]+=joint[k]*joint[k];}
            }
            for k in 0..2 {
                let mean=sum[k]/n as f64;
                let variance=(squares[k]-sum[k]*sum[k]/n as f64)/(n-1) as f64;
                let standard_error=(variance/n as f64).sqrt();
                assert!((mean-exact[k]).abs()<6.*standard_error+1e-10,
                    "state={k} estimate={mean} exact={} MCSE={standard_error}",exact[k]);
            }
        }
    }
    '"''';p.write_text(s)

    """Exact recorded-flight replay through the canonical ParticleModel root engine."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import concurrent.futures,gzip,hashlib,json,subprocess,sys
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(Path(p).read_text());sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    d=read(B/'replay-design.json')
    for p,h in d['identities'].items():assert sha(p)==h,p
    def execute(j):
     out=B/j['name'];receipt=out.with_suffix('.execution.json')
     if receipt.exists():assert read(receipt)['returncode']==0;return
     assert not out.exists(),out
     with (B/f"{j['name']}-measurement.log").open('w') as log:
      subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(B.parent/'measure.py'),d['binary'],d['model'],j['settings'],str(out)],stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
     print(j['name'],'execution complete',flush=True)
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:list(pool.map(execute,d['jobs'][:2]))
    execute(d['jobs'][2])
    checks=[]
    for j in d['jobs']:
     out=B/j['name'];reference=Path(j['reference']);records=0;leaves=0;identities={}
     for old in sorted(reference.glob('roots-*.json')):
      new=out/old.name;a=read(old);b=read(new);assert len(a)==len(b)
      for x,y in zip(a,b):
       for key in ['root','initial','terminal_rows','evaluated_terminal_rows','omitted_log_mass']:assert x[key]==y[key],(j['name'],x['root'],key)
       assert len(x['stages'])==len(y['stages'])
       for k,(xx,yy) in enumerate(zip(x['stages'],y['stages'])):
        for key,value in yy.items():assert xx[key]==value,(j['name'],x['root'],k,key,xx[key],value)
      records+=len(a);identities[str(old)]=sha(old);identities[str(new)]=sha(new)
     for old in sorted(reference.glob('rows-*.jsonl.gz')):
      new=out/old.name;a=gzip.decompress(old.read_bytes());b=gzip.decompress(new.read_bytes());assert a==b,(j['name'],old.name)
      leaves+=len(a.splitlines());identities[str(old)]=sha(old);identities[str(new)]=sha(new)
     checks.append(dict(name=j['name'],roots=records,terminal_rows=leaves,common_root_statistics_exact=True,terminal_uncompressed_bytes_exact=True,execution=read(out.with_suffix('.execution.json')),identities=identities))
     print(j['name'],records,'roots,',leaves,'terminal rows: exact',flush=True)
    (B/'replay-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(design_sha256=sha(B/'replay-design.json'),checks=checks,scope='The generic canonical ParticleModel engine preserves initial states, every common per-contact root statistic, omitted output mass and all decompressed terminal trajectory bytes, including the influential archived family and a two-thread replay. This is computational equivalence, not new physical validation.'),indent=2))

      $ cat > /tmp/mh370-independent-branching-f3bdfhp5/population.txt <<'EOF'
    /// Complete independent-root population. Leaf weights are normalized globally;
    /// diagnostic root masses retain the configured computational scales. Every
    /// declared root, including extinct trees, remains in "'`roots`.
    #[derive(Debug)]
    pub struct BranchingPopulation<S> {
        pub particles: Vec<S>,
        pub log_weights: Vec<f64>,
        pub root_ids: Vec<usize>,
        pub leaf_ids: Vec<usize>,
        pub roots: Vec<BranchingRootDiagnostics>,
        pub checkpoints: Vec<crate::FilterCheckpoint>,
        pub log_evidence: f64,
    }

    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
    pub struct BranchingRootDiagnostics {
        pub root: usize,
        pub stratum: StratumId,
        pub stages: Vec<BranchingStage>,
        pub terminal_states: usize,
    }

    /// Repeat the smallest integer allocation cycle. This preserves root identities
    /// when a fixed allocation is extended by an integer number of cycles. The
    /// within-stratum index remains unique across cycles and is supplied explicitly
    /// to models that stratify their initialization.
    pub fn branching_root_specs(
        plan: &crate::StratifiedFilterPlan,
    ) -> Result<Vec<BranchingRootSpec>, SmcError> {
        let total = plan.strata.iter().try_fold(0usize, |n, s| n.checked_add(s.particles))
            .ok_or(SmcError::InvalidConfiguration("root allocation overflow"))?;
        plan.validate(total).map_err(SmcError::InvalidConfiguration)?;
        fn gcd(mut a: usize, mut b: usize) -> usize {
            while b != 0 { let r = a % b; a = b; b = r; }
            a
        }
        let allocations = plan.canonical_allocations();
        let repetitions = allocations.iter().map(|s| s.particles).reduce(gcd).unwrap();
        let mut specs = Vec::with_capacity(total);
        for cycle in 0..repetitions {
            for s in &allocations {
                let per_cycle = s.particles / repetitions;
                // Preserve the validated implementation'"'s arithmetic order.
                let correction = (s.log_prior_probability.exp() * total as f64 / s.particles as f64).ln();
                for within in 0..per_cycle {
                    specs.push(BranchingRootSpec {
                        index: specs.len(), stratum: s.id,
                        index_within_stratum: cycle * per_cycle + within,
                        stratum_population: s.particles,
                        log_stratum_correction: correction,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(specs)
    }

    /// Evaluate a fixed population without interaction between root trees. Parallel
    /// work is collected in root order; no partial population is returned on error.
    /// Batches bound transient memory, not scientific population size. Every finite
    /// terminal state is retained, with no posterior-dependent pruning or leaf cap.
    pub fn run_branching_population<M: ParticleModel>(
        model: &M,
        observations: &[M::Observation],
        config: &BranchingConfig,
        plan: &crate::StratifiedFilterPlan,
    ) -> Result<BranchingPopulation<M::State>, SmcError> {
        use rayon::prelude::*;
        validate_branching(model, observations, config)?;
        let specs = branching_root_specs(plan)?;
        let mut result = BranchingPopulation {
            particles: Vec::new(), log_weights: Vec::new(), root_ids: Vec::new(),
            leaf_ids: Vec::new(), roots: Vec::with_capacity(specs.len()),
            checkpoints: Vec::new(), log_evidence: 0.,
        };
        for batch in specs.chunks(256) {
            let evaluated: Vec<_> = batch.par_iter().map(|&spec| {
                let mut leaves = Vec::new();
                let root = run_branching_root(model, observations, config, spec,
                    |leaf, state, weight, _, _, _| {
                        leaves.push((leaf, state.clone(), weight)); Ok(())
                    })?;
                Ok((BranchingRootDiagnostics { root: spec.index, stratum: spec.stratum,
                    stages: root.stages, terminal_states: root.terminal_states }, leaves))
            }).collect::<Result<_, SmcError>>()?;
            for (root, leaves) in evaluated {
                for (leaf, state, weight) in leaves {
                    result.particles.push(state);
                    result.log_weights.push(weight);
                    result.root_ids.push(root.root);
                    result.leaf_ids.push(leaf);
                }
                result.roots.push(root);
            }
        }
        let ids: Vec<_> = result.roots.iter().map(|r| r.root).collect();
        let strata: Vec<_> = result.roots.iter().map(|r| r.stratum).collect();
        let mut scale = 0.;
        let mut previous_evidence = 0.;
        for (k, observation) in observations.iter().enumerate() {
            scale += config.log_likelihood_scales[k];
            let masses: Vec<_> = result.roots.iter().map(|r| r.stages[k].log_mass).collect();
            let log_mass = logsumexp(&masses)?;
            let evidence = log_mass - (specs.len() as f64).ln() - scale;
            let mut diagnostics = crate::filter::weight_diagnostics(&masses, &ids, &strata)?;
            // "'`weight_diagnostics` above operates on family sums. Restore leaf-level
            // diagnostics from sufficient statistics; family ESS stays unchanged.
            let square_mass = result.roots.iter().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY,
                |a, r| log_add(a, r.stages[k].log_square_mass));
            let maximum = result.roots.iter().map(|r| r.stages[k].max_log_weight)
                .fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
            for s in &mut diagnostics.strata {
                let members: Vec<_> = result.roots.iter().filter(|r| r.stratum == s.id)
                    .map(|r| &r.stages[k]).collect();
                s.particle_count = members.iter().map(|r| r.supported as usize).sum();
                if let Some(stratum_log_mass) = s.log_posterior_mass {
                    let absolute_mass = log_mass + stratum_log_mass;
                    let squares = members.iter().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, |a, r| log_add(a, r.log_square_mass));
                    let max = members.iter().map(|r| r.max_log_weight).fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
                    s.conditional_ess = (2. * absolute_mass - squares).exp();
                    s.maximum_normalized_log_weight = Some(max - log_mass);
                    s.maximum_normalized_weight = (max - log_mass).exp();
                }
            }
            result.checkpoints.push(crate::FilterCheckpoint {
                observation_index: k, observation_time_s: model.observation_time_s(observation),
                guide_ess: None, posterior_ess: (2. * log_mass - square_mass).exp(),
                ancestor_resampled: false, posterior_resampled: false,
                log_evidence_increment: evidence - previous_evidence,
                cumulative_log_evidence: evidence,
                maximum_normalized_weight: (maximum - log_mass).exp(),
                distinct_root_ancestors: diagnostics.distinct_root_ancestors,
                root_effective_sample_size: diagnostics.root_effective_sample_size,
                maximum_root_weight: diagnostics.maximum_root_weight,
                roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_6: diagnostics.roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_6,
                roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_3: diagnostics.roots_with_mass_at_least_1e_3,
                strata: diagnostics.strata,
            });
            previous_evidence = evidence;
        }
        result.log_evidence = previous_evidence;
        result.log_weights = crate::normalize_log_weights(&result.log_weights)?.0;
        Ok(result)
    }

    EOF

Optional exact display replay for independent cruise root trees. use crate::write_json; use anyhow::{bail, Result}; use mh370_dynamics::CruiseSegment; use mh370_estimator::{CruiseFilterModel, CruiseParticle, FlightObservation}; use mh370_particle_filter::{     branching_root_specs, rng_for, run_branching_root, BranchingConfig, BranchingPopulation,     Initialization, ParticleModel, Proposal, SmcError, StratifiedFilterPlan, StratumId, }; use rand::Rng; use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng; use std::{collections::BTreeMap, path::Path, sync::Mutex}; /// Depth-first traversal needs only the current path'"'s trace. This wrapper is /// used during display replay, never during inference or weight calculation. struct TraceModel<'a, 'b> {     model: &'a CruiseFilterModel<'b>,     observations: &'a [FlightObservation],     initial: Mutex<Option<CruiseParticle>>,     segments: Mutex<Vec<Vec<CruiseSegment>>>, } impl ParticleModel for TraceModel<'_, '_> {     type State = CruiseParticle;     type Observation = FlightObservation;     type Error = SmcError;     fn observation_time_s(&self, o: &FlightObservation) -> f64 { self.model.observation_time_s(o) }     fn stratum(&self, s: &CruiseParticle) -> StratumId { self.model.stratum(s) }     fn initialize(&self, i: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<CruiseParticle, SmcError> {         self.model.initialize(i, rng).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))     }     fn initialize_in_stratum(&self, s: StratumId, i: usize, j: usize, n: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng)         -> Result<Initialization<CruiseParticle>, SmcError> {         let value = self.model.initialize_in_stratum(s, i, j, n, rng)             .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;         *self.initial.lock().unwrap() = Some(value.state.clone());         Ok(value)     }     fn propose(&self, s: &CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation, _: f64, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng)         -> Result<Proposal<CruiseParticle>, SmcError> {         let k = self.observations.iter().position(|candidate| std::ptr::eq(candidate, o))             .ok_or(SmcError::InvalidAncestry)?;         let mut trace = Vec::new();         let value = self.model.propose_with_trace(s, o, rng, &mut trace)             .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;         let mut path = self.segments.lock().unwrap();         if path.len() < k { return Err(SmcError::InvalidAncestry); }         path.truncate(k);         path.push(trace);         Ok(value)     }     fn observe(&self, s: &mut CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {         self.model.observe(s, o).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))     }     fn log_likelihood(&self, s: &CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {         self.model.log_likelihood(s, o).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))     } } pub(crate) fn export_examples(     model: &CruiseFilterModel<'_>, observations: &[FlightObservation], config: &BranchingConfig,     plan: &StratifiedFilterPlan, result: &BranchingPopulation<CruiseParticle>,     examples_per_bin: usize, requested: &[usize], output: &Path, ) -> Result<()> {     if requested.iter().any(|&i| i >= result.particles.len() || "'!result.log_weights[i].is_finite()) {         bail!("requested trajectory example has no finite-weight terminal row");
