      $ "cat > crates/runner/src/cruise_branching.rs <<'EOF'
    //"'! Optional exact display replay for independent cruise root trees.
    use crate::write_json;
    use anyhow::{bail, Result};
    use mh370_dynamics::CruiseSegment;
    use mh370_estimator::{CruiseFilterModel, CruiseParticle, FlightObservation};
    use mh370_particle_filter::{
        branching_root_specs, rng_for, run_branching_root, BranchingConfig, BranchingPopulation,
        Initialization, ParticleModel, Proposal, SmcError, StratifiedFilterPlan, StratumId,
    };
    use rand::Rng;
    use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;
    use std::{collections::BTreeMap, path::Path, sync::Mutex};

    /// Depth-first traversal needs only the current path'"'s trace. This wrapper is
    /// used during display replay, never during inference or weight calculation.
    struct TraceModel<'a, 'b> {
        model: &'a CruiseFilterModel<'b>,
        observations: &'a [FlightObservation],
        initial: Mutex<Option<CruiseParticle>>,
        segments: Mutex<Vec<Vec<CruiseSegment>>>,
    }
    impl ParticleModel for TraceModel<'_, '_> {
        type State = CruiseParticle;
        type Observation = FlightObservation;
        type Error = SmcError;
        fn observation_time_s(&self, o: &FlightObservation) -> f64 { self.model.observation_time_s(o) }
        fn stratum(&self, s: &CruiseParticle) -> StratumId { self.model.stratum(s) }
        fn initialize(&self, i: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<CruiseParticle, SmcError> {
            self.model.initialize(i, rng).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))
        }
        fn initialize_in_stratum(&self, s: StratumId, i: usize, j: usize, n: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng)
            -> Result<Initialization<CruiseParticle>, SmcError> {
            let value = self.model.initialize_in_stratum(s, i, j, n, rng)
                .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
            *self.initial.lock().unwrap() = Some(value.state.clone());
            Ok(value)
        }
        fn propose(&self, s: &CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation, _: f64, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng)
            -> Result<Proposal<CruiseParticle>, SmcError> {
            let k = self.observations.iter().position(|candidate| std::ptr::eq(candidate, o))
                .ok_or(SmcError::InvalidAncestry)?;
            let mut trace = Vec::new();
            let value = self.model.propose_with_trace(s, o, rng, &mut trace)
                .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
            let mut path = self.segments.lock().unwrap();
            if path.len() < k { return Err(SmcError::InvalidAncestry); }
            path.truncate(k);
            path.push(trace);
            Ok(value)
        }
        fn observe(&self, s: &mut CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {
            self.model.observe(s, o).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))
        }
        fn log_likelihood(&self, s: &CruiseParticle, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {
            self.model.log_likelihood(s, o).map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn export_examples(
        model: &CruiseFilterModel<'_>, observations: &[FlightObservation], config: &BranchingConfig,
        plan: &StratifiedFilterPlan, result: &BranchingPopulation<CruiseParticle>,
        examples_per_bin: usize, requested: &[usize], output: &Path,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        if requested.iter().any(|&i| i >= result.particles.len() || "'!result.log_weights[i].is_finite()) {
            bail!("requested trajectory example has no finite-weight terminal row");
        }
        let weights: Vec<_> = result.log_weights.iter().map(|w| w.exp()).collect();
        let mut by_bin: BTreeMap<i32, Vec<usize>> = BTreeMap::new();
        for (i, (p, w)) in result.particles.iter().zip(&weights).enumerate() {
            if *w > 0. { by_bin.entry((p.flight.aircraft.position.latitude.0 / 2.5).round() as i32)
                .or_default().push(i); }
        }
        let mut rng = rng_for(config.seed, "trajectory-display-selection", 0, 0, 0);
        let mut bins = Vec::new();
        let mut selected_by_root: BTreeMap<usize, Vec<(usize, i32, f64)>> = BTreeMap::new();
        for (bin, indices) in by_bin {
            let bin_mass: f64 = indices.iter().map(|&i| weights[i]).sum();
            let mut remaining = indices.clone();
            let mut selected = Vec::new();
            for _ in 0..examples_per_bin.min(remaining.len()) {
                let target = rng.gen::<f64>() * remaining.iter().map(|&i| weights[i]).sum::<f64>();
                let mut cumulative = 0.;
                let position = remaining.iter().position(|&i| { cumulative += weights[i]; cumulative > target })
                    .unwrap_or(remaining.len() - 1);
                selected.push(remaining.remove(position));
            }
            for &i in requested { if indices.contains(&i) && !selected.contains(&i) { selected.push(i); } }
            bins.push(serde_json::json!({"latitude_bin_center_deg":f64::from(bin)*2.5,
                "latitude_bin_width_deg":2.5,"probability_in_source_run":bin_mass,
                "positive_weight_rows":indices.len(),"selected_terminal_slots":selected}));
            for slot in selected { selected_by_root.entry(result.root_ids[slot]).or_default().push((slot, bin, bin_mass)); }
        }
        let specs = branching_root_specs(plan)?;
        let log_total = result.log_evidence + (specs.len() as f64).ln()
            + config.log_likelihood_scales.iter().sum::<f64>();
        let mut examples = Vec::new();
        let expected_count: usize = selected_by_root.values().map(Vec::len).sum();
        for (root, selected) in selected_by_root {
            let tracing = TraceModel { model, observations, initial: Mutex::new(None), segments: Mutex::new(Vec::new()) };
            let replay = run_branching_root(&tracing, observations, config, specs[root],
                |leaf, terminal, weight, branch_path, history, roulette| {
                    let matching = selected.iter().find(|(slot, _, _)| result.leaf_ids[*slot] == leaf);
                    let Some(&(slot, bin, bin_mass)) = matching else { return Ok(()); };
                    if serde_json::to_vec(terminal).unwrap() != serde_json::to_vec(&result.particles[slot]).unwrap()
                        || (weight - log_total - result.log_weights[slot]).abs() > 1e-10 {
                        return Err(SmcError::Model("exact independent-root trajectory replay mismatch".into()));
                    }
                    let initial = tracing.initial.lock().unwrap();
                    let initial = initial.as_ref().ok_or(SmcError::InvalidAncestry)?;
                    let a = initial.flight.aircraft;
                    let mut points = vec![serde_json::json!({"time_s":a.time.0,"latitude_deg":a.position.latitude.0,
                        "longitude_deg":a.position.longitude.0,"altitude_ft":a.altitude.0,
                        "mach":initial.flight.mach_set+initial.flight.mach_deviation})];
                    let segments = tracing.segments.lock().unwrap();
                    if segments.len() != history.len() { return Err(SmcError::InvalidAncestry); }
                    for segment in segments.iter().flatten() {
                        points.push(serde_json::json!({"time_s":segment.end_time.0,
                            "latitude_deg":segment.end_position.latitude.0,"longitude_deg":segment.end_position.longitude.0,
                            "altitude_ft":segment.end_altitude_ft,"mach":segment.end_mach,
                            "bank_magnitude_deg":segment.bank_deg,"vertical_speed_ft_min":segment.vertical_speed_ft_min}));
                    }
                    let contacts: Vec<_> = history.iter().zip(observations).map(|(state, observation)|
                        serde_json::json!({"time_s":state.flight.aircraft.time.0,"state":state,"observation":observation})).collect();
                    examples.push(serde_json::json!({"seed":config.seed,"terminal_slot":slot,"root":root,"leaf":leaf,
                        "latitude_bin_center_deg":f64::from(bin)*2.5,"source_run_weight":weights[slot],
                        "bin_probability_in_source_run":bin_mass,"terminal":terminal,"initial":initial,
                        "branch_path":branch_path,"roulette_contacts":roulette,"points":points,"contacts":contacts}));
                    Ok(())
                })?;
            let expected = &result.roots[root];
            if replay.terminal_states != expected.terminal_states
                || replay.stages.iter().zip(&expected.stages).any(|(a,b)|
                    a.attempts != b.attempts || a.log_mass.to_bits() != b.log_mass.to_bits()) {
                bail!("independent-root replay changed root statistics");
            }
        }
        if examples.len() != expected_count { bail!("not every selected trajectory was recovered"); }
        write_json(&output.join("trajectory-examples.json"), &serde_json::json!({
            "seed":config.seed,"latitude_bin_width_deg":2.5,"examples_per_nonempty_bin":examples_per_bin,
            "selection":"posterior-weighted display sampling without replacement within each latitude bin, plus explicitly requested retained row identities; no inference weights changed",
            "requested_terminal_slots":requested,"scope":"exact selected histories from this source replicate, not additional independent candidates or an all-posterior contact population",
            "exact_terminal_and_root_statistics_match":true,"bins":bins,"examples":examples,
        }))
    }

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/runner-integration/verify_cli.py <<'PY'
    """Exact source replay plus independently reconstructed population accounting."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import gzip,hashlib,json,math
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.special import logsumexp
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(p.read_text())
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    ref=B/'fresh-root-replay';out=B/'cli-replay';r=read(out/'particles.json')
    oldroots=[x for p in sorted(ref.glob('roots-*.json')) for x in read(p)]
    newroots=read(out/'root-diagnostics.json')
    assert len(oldroots)==len(newroots)==2000
    for old,new in zip(oldroots,newroots):
     assert old['root']==new['root']
     assert old['evaluated_terminal_rows']==new['terminal_states'],old['root']
     assert old['stages']==new['stages'],old['root']
    rows=[]
    for p in sorted(ref.glob('rows-*.jsonl.gz')):
     with gzip.open(p,'rt') as f:rows.extend(json.loads(line) for line in f)
    indices={(root,leaf):i for i,(root,leaf) in enumerate(zip(r['root_ids'],r['leaf_ids']))}
    assert len(indices)==len(r['particles'])
    scales=read(ref/'likelihood-scales.json')['log_scales']
    rootmasses=np.array([x['stages'][-1]['log_mass'] if x['stages'][-1]['log_mass'] is not None else -np.inf for x in oldroots])
    logtotal=float(logsumexp(rootmasses));z=logtotal-math.log(2000)-sum(scales)
    assert abs(r['log_evidence']-z)<1e-12
    max_weight_error=0.
    for row in rows:
     i=indices[(row['root'],row['leaf'])];p=r['particles'][i];f=p['flight'];a=f['aircraft'];v=row['states'][-1]
     assert [a['position']['latitude'],a['position']['longitude'],a['altitude'],f['mach_set']+f['mach_deviation'],a['track_true'],a['ground_speed']]==v[:6],(row['root'],row['leaf'])
     assert p['bfo_bias']['mean_hz']==row['bias_mean']
     assert p['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2']==row['bias_variance']
     assert p['cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood']==row['cumulative_log_likelihood']
     assert f['tau_hours']==row['tau_hours'] and f['manoeuvre_counts']==row['counts']
     max_weight_error=max(max_weight_error,abs(r['log_weights'][i]-(row['log_weight']-logtotal)))
    assert max_weight_error<1e-12
    assert abs(float(logsumexp(r['log_weights'])))<1e-12
    for k,c in enumerate(r['checkpoints']):
     masses=np.array([x['stages'][k]['log_mass'] if x['stages'][k]['log_mass'] is not None else -np.inf for x in oldroots])
     weights=np.exp(masses-logsumexp(masses))
     assert abs(c['root_effective_sample_size']-1/float(weights@weights))<1e-9
     assert abs(c['maximum_root_weight']-float(max(weights)))<1e-12
     assert c['distinct_root_ancestors']==int(np.isfinite(masses).sum())
     assert abs(c['cumulative_log_evidence']-(float(logsumexp(masses))-math.log(2000)-sum(scales[:k+1])))<1e-12
    examples=read(out/'trajectory-examples.json');rowmap={(x['root'],x['leaf']):x for x in rows}
    for e in examples['examples']:
     row=rowmap[(e['root'],e['leaf'])]
     assert len(e['contacts'])==10
     for contact,v in zip(e['contacts'],row['states']):
      f=contact['state']['flight'];a=f['aircraft']
      assert [a['position']['latitude'],a['position']['longitude'],a['altitude'],f['mach_set']+f['mach_deviation'],a['track_true'],a['ground_speed']]==v[:6]
     assert e['branch_path']==row['branch_path']
     assert e['points'][0]['time_s']==0 and e['points'][-1]['time_s']==22150
     assert len(e['points'])>1000
    extra=[i for key,i in indices.items() if key not in rowmap]
    extra_mass=sum(math.exp(r['log_weights'][i]) for i in extra)
    result=dict(root_stage_statistics_exact=True,archived_terminal_rows_exact=len(rows),all_terminal_rows=len(indices),additional_rows_below_archived_output_cutoff=len(extra),additional_normalized_mass=extra_mass,maximum_normalized_log_weight_rounding=max_weight_error,independent_checkpoint_recalculation=True,exact_all_contact_example_replays=len(examples['examples']),log_evidence=r['log_evidence'],identities={str(p):sha(p) for p in [B/'cli-replay-config.json',B/'cli-replay.execution.json',out/'particles.json',out/'root-diagnostics.json',out/'trajectory-examples.json']})
    (B/'cli-verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
    PY

    # Independent-root runner integration

    Work in progress. The engine is being integrated in an isolated copy of the current workspace. It has not yet replaced or modified the shared runner. The scientific target remains the frozen uncapped cruise/SATCOM model through 00:10:59.

    The single-root generic "'`ParticleModel` engine reproduced 2,000 fresh roots and 100 roots containing the influential archived family. All common root/contact statistics and all decompressed terminal trajectory bytes matched, including a two-thread replay. See [replay results](replay-results.json). The verified source is preserved in `root-engine-replay-source.tar.gz`, with identities in `root-engine-replay-source.json`; the isolated source is now advancing beyond that archived snapshot.

    The population layer preserves every declared root, including extinct trees, applies physical-prior/computational-allocation corrections, and collects parallel results in deterministic root order. Root-family ESS and maximum family mass are separate from terminal-leaf ESS. Every finite terminal state is retained; the old output-only cutoff is not applied. Scientific equations stay in the existing model spokes.

    Five focused engine tests pass: independently enumerated evidence with a biased prior proposal, nonunit-threshold roulette mass conservation, zero support versus invalid weights/output failure, an independently enumerated hidden Markov model with biased transition proposals, and unequal population allocations with independent evidence/family calculations and thread-order invariance. See `population-tests.log`.

    Next checks are declared in [population integration design](population-integration-design.json): a complete CLI replay of the 2,000-root fixture, independent recalculation of its evidence/checkpoints, exact all-contact replay of selected illustrative trajectories, and an unchanged sequential-runner replay. Selected trajectories are display samples, not a substitute for all-posterior ancestral states for additional evidence.

    These checks establish implementation equivalence and numerical accounting. They do not establish stable inference, physical calibration, or exact reproduction of all unpublished inputs used by Davey et al. The [scaling results](../extended-scaling/REPORT.md), [history-inclusive comparison](../aggregate-reference/REPORT.md), and [known-flight checks](../known-flight-scaling/design.json) remain separate evidence about those questions.
    EOF'

      def read_run(path):
          summary = json.loads((path / 'summary.json').read_text())
          config = json.loads((path / 'resolved-config.json').read_text())
          if 'sampling' in config:
              config['branching'] = config['sampling']  # archived report metadata key
          provenance = json.loads((path / 'provenance.json').read_text())
          if not summary.get('posterior_available'):
              raise ValueError(f'No completed estimate in {path}')
          if digest(path / 'posterior.csv') != summary['posterior_csv_sha256']:
              raise ValueError(f'Posterior checksum mismatch: {path}')
          with (path / 'posterior.csv').open() as f:
              rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
          xy = np.array([[float(r[k]) for k in ['latitude_deg', 'longitude_deg']] for r in rows])
          w = np.array([float(r['weight']) for r in rows])
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)) or np.any(w < 0) or abs(w.sum() - 1) > 1e-8:
              raise ValueError('Invalid saved probability weights')
          w /= w.sum()
          return dict(path=path, summary=summary, config=config, provenance=provenance,
                      points=xy, weight=w, rows=rows,
                      initial_modes=np.array([r['initial_mode'] for r in rows]),
                      final_modes=np.array([r['final_mode'] for r in rows]))


      def physical_identity(run):
          cfg = run['config']
          model = json.loads(json.dumps(cfg['model']))
          model.pop('proposal_guide', None)
          model['dynamics'].pop('marginalize_manoeuvre_rate', None)
          return dict(model=model, inputs=run['provenance']['inputs'], modes=cfg['modes'],
                      mode_probabilities=cfg['mode_probabilities'], input_settings=cfg['inputs'],
                      scientific_source=run['provenance']['scientific_source_sha256'])


      def mix_weights(runs, equal=False):
          effort=np.array([r['summary']['initial_particles'] for r in runs],dtype=float)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(effort)) or np.any(effort<=0):
              raise ValueError('Runs must record positive initial effort')
          logz = np.array([r['summary']['log_evidence'] for r in runs])
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(logz)):
              raise ValueError('Missing finite normalizer estimate')
          weights = np.ones(len(runs)) if equal else effort*np.exp(logz-logz.max())
          return weights / weights.sum()


      def pool(runs, equal=False):
          alpha = mix_weights(runs, equal)
          return (np.concatenate([r['points'] for r in runs]),
                  np.concatenate([r['weight'] * a for r, a in zip(runs, alpha)]))


      def quantiles(points, weights, ps=(.05, .5, .95)):
          out = []
          for col in range(2):
              order = np.argsort(points[:, col])
              cumulative = np.cumsum(weights[order])
              out.append(points[order, col][np.searchsorted(cumulative, ps).clip(max=len(order)-1)].tolist())
          return out


      def cdf_difference(first, second):
          out = []

      //! Embed the source identity of the scientific model separately from the
      //! sampling engine, so controlled algorithm comparisons can share a target.
      use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
      use std::{
          fs,
          path::{Path, PathBuf},
      };

      fn collect(directory: &Path, files: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) {
          for entry in fs::read_dir(directory).expect("scientific source directory") {
              let path = entry.expect("source entry").path();
              if path.is_dir() {
                  collect(&path, files);
              } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "rs") {
                  files.push(path);
              }
          }
      }

      fn main() {
          let manifest = PathBuf::from(std::env::var("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR").unwrap());
          let workspace = manifest.parent().unwrap().parent().unwrap();
          let mut files = vec![workspace.join("Cargo.lock")];
          for name in ["domain", "dynamics", "satcom", "end-of-flight", "estimator"] {
              let directory = workspace.join("crates").join(name).join("src");
              println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed={}", directory.display());
              collect(&directory, &mut files);
          }
          files.sort();
          let mut digest = Sha256::new();
          for path in files {
              println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed={}", path.display());
              let relative = path.strip_prefix(workspace).unwrap().to_string_lossy();
              digest.update(relative.as_bytes());
              digest.update([0]);
              digest.update(fs::read(&path).unwrap());
              digest.update([0]);
          }
          println!(
              "cargo:rustc-env=MH370_SCIENTIFIC_SOURCE_SHA256={:x}",
              digest.finalize()
          );
      }

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/runner-integration/measure_runner.py <<'PY'
    """Measure one runner child with wait4, excluding inherited shell/build counters."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import hashlib,json,os,resource,subprocess,sys,time
    binary,config,out,threads,limit=sys.argv[1:];out=Path(out)
    assert not out.exists(),out
    cmd=[binary,'estimate-cruise','--config',config,'--output',str(out),'--threads',threads]
    identities={str(Path(p)):hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in [binary,config]}
    def bounds():
     if int(limit)>0:resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU,(int(limit),int(limit)+10))
    t=time.perf_counter()
    with out.with_suffix('.log').open('w') as f:
     child=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,preexec_fn=bounds)
     pid,status,u=os.wait4(child.pid,0);child.returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
    receipt=dict(command=cmd,returncode=child.returncode,wall_seconds=time.perf_counter()-t,cpu_seconds=u.ru_utime+u.ru_stime,maxrss_kib=u.ru_maxrss,identities=identities,measurement='per-child wait4; excludes preceding compiler and shell children')
    out.with_suffix('.execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(receipt,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(receipt),flush=True)
    sys.exit(child.returncode)

    cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/core-filter-scaling/runner-integration/shared_controls.py <<'PY'
    from pathlib import Path
    import concurrent.futures,hashlib,json,shutil,subprocess,sys
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    binary=B/'canonical-runner';assert not binary.exists()
    shutil.copyfile(B.parent/'control-target/iteration/mh370',binary);binary.chmod(0o755)
    jobs=[('canonical-replay',2,600),('known-flight-replay',1,900)]
    dict_sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (B/'shared-control-design.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(binary_sha256=dict_sha(binary),configs={name:dict_sha(B/(name+'-config.json')) for name,_,_ in jobs},measurement_sha256=dict_sha(B/'measure_runner.py'),scope='Portable shared runner replay after progress reporting; MH370 2,000 roots and MH371 8,000 roots.'),indent=2)+'\n')
    def run(job):
     name,threads,limit=job
     return subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(B/'measure_runner.py'),str(binary),str(B/(name+'-config.json')),str(B/name),str(threads),str(limit)],check=True).returncode
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:list(pool.map(run,jobs))
    print('Shared control runs ready for verification',flush=True)

    from pathlib import Path
    import gzip,hashlib,json,math
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.special import logsumexp
    B=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    read=lambda p:json.loads(p.read_text())
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    a=B/'canonical-replay';b=B/'cli-replay'
    for name in ['posterior.csv','root-diagnostics.json','trajectory-examples.json']:
     assert (a/name).read_bytes()==(b/name).read_bytes(),name
    x=read(a/'particles.json');y=read(b/'particles.json')
    for key in x:
     if key"'!='"'elapsed_seconds':assert x[key]==y[key],key
    progress=read(a/'sampling-progress.json');assert progress['completed_roots']==progress['planned_roots']==2000
    old=B.parent/'defensive-command-mixture/known-flight/runs/original-8000-seed-20260925110'
    new=B/'known-flight-replay';roots=read(new/'root-diagnostics.json');p=read(new/'particles.json')
    reference=[r for f in sorted(old.glob('roots-*.json')) for r in read(f)]
    assert len(reference)==len(roots)==8000
    max_stage_error=0.;all_stage_exact=True
    for a,b in zip(reference,roots):
     assert a['root']==b['root'] and a['evaluated_terminal_rows']==b['terminal_states']
     for x,y in zip(a['stages'],b['stages']):
      for key,value in y.items():
       if key in ['log_mass','log_square_mass','max_log_weight'] and value is not None:
        error=abs(x[key]-value);max_stage_error=max(max_stage_error,error);all_stage_exact &= error==0
       else:assert x[key]==value,(a['root'],key)
    assert max_stage_error<1e-12
    indices={(root,leaf):i for i,(root,leaf) in enumerate(zip(p['root_ids'],p['leaf_ids']))}
    rows=[]
    for f in sorted(old.glob('rows-*.jsonl.gz')):
     with gzip.open(f,'rt') as stream:rows.extend(json.loads(line) for line in stream)
    logtotal=float(logsumexp([r['stages'][-1]['log_mass'] if r['stages'][-1]['log_mass'] is not None else -np.inf for r in reference]));max_weight_error=0.
    for row in rows:
     i=indices[(row['root'],row['leaf'])];s=p['particles'][i];f=s['flight'];a=f['aircraft'];v=row['states'][-1]
     assert [a['position']['latitude'],a['position']['longitude'],a['altitude'],f['mach_set']+f['mach_deviation'],a['track_true'],a['ground_speed']]==v[:6],(row['root'],row['leaf'])
     assert s['bfo_bias']['mean_hz']==row['bias_mean'] and s['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2']==row['bias_variance']
     assert s['cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood']==row['cumulative_log_likelihood']
     max_weight_error=max(max_weight_error,abs(p['log_weights'][i]-(row['log_weight']-logtotal)))
    assert max_weight_error<1e-12
    scale=sum(read(old/'likelihood-scales.json')['log_scales']);assert abs(p['log_evidence']-(logtotal-math.log(8000)-scale))<1e-12
    progress=read(new/'sampling-progress.json');assert progress['completed_roots']==progress['planned_roots']==8000
    result=dict(portable_mh370_scientific_outputs_byte_exact=True,known_flight_archived_roots=8000,known_flight_exact_terminal_states=len(rows),known_flight_stage_statistics_exact=all_stage_exact,known_flight_maximum_stage_log_weight_difference=max_stage_error,known_flight_maximum_normalized_log_weight_rounding=max_weight_error,known_flight_all_finite_terminal_states=len(indices),progress_counts_complete=True,controls={name:read(B/(name+'.execution.json')) for name in ['canonical-replay','known-flight-replay']},identities={str(f):sha(f) for f in [B/'canonical-tests.log',B/'shared-control-design.json',B/'canonical-runner']})
    (B/'shared-verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
